歐洲股市：歐洲央行態度謹慎，川普加徵歐洲汽車關稅
4 月歐股受能源價格大漲衝擊，雖財報優異但科技股比重相對美股較低，泛歐 600 指數緩步墊高。
指數上揚主要由艾司摩爾、西門子、西門子能源、匯豐、艾波比、英飛凌、施耐德電機、諾基亞、瑞
銀集團、諾和諾德等企業帶動，表現相對弱勢的族群包括殼牌、德意志電信、阿斯特捷利康、諾華、
葛蘭素史克、聯合利華、依視路羅薩奧蒂卡、赫力昂、挪威國家石油、BP 等，市場焦點已經從能源、
國防題材回歸科技與金融股。
4 月歐元區 服務業受到物價飆漲與能源支出暴增的雙重打擊， 服務 業採購經理人指數 急挫至

47.4，創疫情後最差表現；然而製造業採購經理人指數則在新訂單改善(尤其法國)及避險備貨需求的
支撐下，逆勢攀升至 52.2，然而就業人數持續萎縮，反映企業在高通膨與需求不確定性下普遍採取
縮編策略，經濟放緩格局明顯。法國製造業 PMI 攀升至 52.8，主要得益於新訂單走高，反映需求復
甦；德國製造業 PMI 雖達 51.4，增長動能依賴企業拉升庫存，能否延續仍須觀察。
儘管歐洲央行在 4 月利率決策會議按兵不動，但此舉是反映需要更多時間凝聚升息共識，並蒐集
能源衝擊對通膨的實際影響數據 。歐洲央行總裁透露委員會內部曾認真討論升息的可能性，維持不動
的理由包括：近期數據仍接近3 月預測、貨幣政策在能源價格上漲前已處於適當位置、金融與信貸條
件已開始收緊，目前局勢「偏離」原先基準情境，通膨上行風險與經濟成長下行風險均在加劇，但歐
洲經濟尚未進入停滯。市場預期 ECB 將於 2026 年升息 2 碼，甚至不排除於年底或於明年升息第 3
碼，但考量歐洲通膨仍與伊朗戰事息息相關，若能源價格回落、需求走弱，則升息時點可能延後；若

能源價格繼續在高檔盤旋、通膨持續性跡象提早浮現，不排除6 月即會啟動第一次升息。英格蘭銀行
(英國央行)在最新利率決策會議中維持利率不變，但整體立場偏向謹慎，市場同樣預期未來半年可能
有 2 碼升息幅度。近期 ECB 與 BOE 的升息預期已帶動歐元、英鎊轉強。
川普於 5/1 宣布，由於歐盟不遵守貿易協議，將對歐盟進口汽車與卡車加徵 25%關稅(不包含英
國)。由於歐洲議會在 3/26 已有條件通過歐美貿易協議的相關立法，故川普調整汽車業關稅從 15%
到 25%，可能引發美歐貿易戰再次啟動，接下來預料歐盟祭出關稅反制措施且展開新一輪談判。
泛歐 600 指數目前本益比為 16.87，股價淨值比為 2.39，目前面臨能源價格仍在高檔、川普對歐
盟汽車業加徵關稅，短期風險增加。然而考量評價合理、伊朗戰爭持續降溫且預料美歐經過談判，終
將能達成貿易協議，歐股仍可以長線角度、作為分批佈局標的。

日本股市：日本利率按兵不動，AI 題材帶動指數創高
4 月上旬日股受地緣政治風險衝擊，所幸在 AI 題材帶動下科技股走高，日經指數更於 4 月一度
收盤站上 60,537 點，以收盤價站上 6 萬點歷史新高。市場主要由軟銀、愛德萬測試、Keyence、村
田製作所、三菱電機、三菱 UFJ 金融、東京威力科創、藤倉、鎧俠、三井住友金融等公司帶動，表
現相對弱勢的族群包括三菱商事、豐田汽車、任天堂、三井物產、永旺、武田藥品、國際石油開發帝
石、7&I、山之內製藥、東方樂園等，市場焦點回歸 AI、國防，以及升息可能帶動的金融股。

相較於歐洲汽車出口至美國關稅再度被川普調高，目前並未傳出美國可能調高日本進口汽車關稅
的訊息，顯示高市早苗先前與川普會晤帶來實質外交成效。日本進入黃金週，但高市首相選擇飛往越
南與澳洲「拼外交」，尤其將澳洲定位為「準同盟」 ，推動以「最上級」護衛艦為基礎的共同開發與輸
出，顯然高市對於國防自主有堅定決心。
日本央行 4 月利率決策會議以 6 比 3 同意將利率維持於 0.75%，三位委員認為應將政策利率調
高至 1.0%，市場對後續升息的預期明顯升溫。日本央行同時將 FY2026 核心 CPI 預測大幅上調 0.9
個百分點至+2.8%，FY2027 上調 0.3 個百分點至+2.3%，這些預測建立在油價將從每桶約 105 美元
回落至 70～80 美元的假設之上 ， 故若油價長期維持高位 ， 通膨前景可能在7 月展望報告中再度上修 ，
而對 GDP 增長預期也小幅下修。對比會後聲明透露出來的鷹派立場，總裁植田和男會後記者會發言
立場則略顯保守，持續淡化央行升息時點，強調需要時間觀察經濟及通膨發展，且提及達成央行經濟

預估的前景下降 ， 尚無法確定需要多久來評估下次升息時點 。 鑒於過於弱勢的日圓匯率可能更進一步
推高國內通膨，日本央行仍有高機率於第三季上調政策利率，若油價再度上行，甚至不排除在 6 月就
採取行動。
截至 4 月 24 日當周，外資買超日股 8,079 億日圓，2026 年以來外資對日股的累計買超金額來
到 7.97 兆日圓，改寫今年以來累計加碼金額紀錄。近期日財長與外匯最高官員相繼釋出干預匯市警
告、加上外資持續匯入且市場對 BOJ 升息的預期，日圓貶值趨勢已扭轉。近期日圓一度單日升值 3%，
顯示 160 為日本政府當局匯價的干預底線，預料近期日圓仍將於 155 至 158 附近震盪。
2026 年以來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材、高市擴大財政刺激、強化軍事安全，但本益比仍
在 19.39 倍，股價淨值比在 1.77 倍。近期日圓守住 160 關卡，匯率穩定可降低投資者不確定性，預
料日本企業股票回購仍持續推進，可望為日股在全球金融市場震盪下帶來支撐。

台灣股市：強勁經濟與產業基本面支撐下的長多格局
4 月台灣股市在 AI 超級週期與全球資金的簇擁下 ， 正式跨越了四萬點 ， 本次台股的突破並非單純
的資金行情，而是具備了極為堅實的總體經濟、強健的產業鏈獲利基本面，以及法規鬆綁帶來的籌碼
結構重塑。
台灣今年初展現了令全球市場矚目的經濟爆發力。主計總處公布的第一季 GDP 成長率概估高達
13.69%，創下近 39 年來的單季最高紀錄。這股強大動能的源頭，清晰地反映在經濟部的三月份外銷
訂單數據上， 三月單月接單金額飆升至 911.2 億美元 ， 年增率高達65.9%， 連續14 個月維持正成長。

資訊通信與電子產品年增率分別高達 120.9%與 73.7%，這顯示台灣在全球 AI 基礎設施、高效能運
算（HPC）以及雲端資料中心建設中，扮演著不可替代的樞紐角色。亮眼的總經數據，為台股的高檔
估值提供了最強而有力的下檔支撐。
中經院 4 月製造業 PMI 大幅跳升 4.9 個百分點至 60.3%，連續 7 個月呈現擴張，更是近 55 個月
以來最快的擴張速度。除了 AI 與半導體的實質需求外，中東地緣政治的緊張局勢促使製造業啟動了
「防禦性提前拉貨」 。短期內這將維持製造業的熱絡，但也必須持續監控輸入性通膨的潛在風險。
宏觀數據之外，台積電於 4 月中旬召開的法說會，無疑是穩住外資與台股軍心的定海神針。財報
顯示，第一季 EPS 達 22.08 元，毛利率以 66.2%飛越財測高標。其中高效能運算（HPC）相關營收
佔比已正式突破六成 （達 61%） ，並上修了2026 全年美元營收成長目標至 30% 以上。這場法說會
不僅確認了台積電高階製程的護城河，也確立了全球 AI 伺服器供應鏈的長線能見度。

在基本面一片大好之際，金管會放寬了主動型基金與主動式 ETF 對單一個股 10%的持股上限，
從 10%放寬至 25%。這項法規鬆綁產生了極為強大的「流動性溢價」效應。過去受限於10%緊箍咒
的機構、 基金資金 ， 如今得以大舉回補台積電 、 聯發科等核心權值股 。這讓大盤指數在衝破四萬點後，
資金高度向權值龍頭集中，成為加權指數的進一步推收力量。
綜合亮眼的總經數據、良好的企業基本面，以及籌碼向大型股集中的法規紅利，我們對台股整體
的長線投資建議維持加碼。然而，目前加權指數與季線的正乖離率已來到 18%的歷史相對高位，意
味著市場隨時可能出現技術性拉回與獲利了結的賣壓。必須強調的是，牛市中的急跌與拉回屬於 「健
康的籌碼換手」 ，並非長線趨勢的反轉。面對當前乖離頗大、但長線看多的盤勢，建議投資人不追高，
待拉回後布局，預留部分現金，保留流動性以迎接拉回時的絕佳買點。

科技類股：半導體產業持續看好，分批佈局雲端巨頭
回顧 4 月份，在地緣政治影響逐漸淡化之後，華爾街對 AI 硬體的「錯失恐懼症（FOMO） 」全面
爆發。費城半導體指數上演了史無前例的連續 18 個交易日上漲，單月狂飆近 40%。這波資金浪潮不
僅將指數推向歷史新高。支撐這波半導體狂飆的是極其扎實的財報利多，不僅英特爾靠著晶圓代工與
CPU 獲利超預期迎來大復活，記憶體族群更步入了史無前例的「超級循環」 。由於AI 伺服器對 HBM
與高階 SSD 需求爆發，導致記憶體陷入結構性大缺貨。美光（Micron）與威騰（WDC）等大廠握

有絕對的定價權，這再次確認了半導體作為「AI 算力基礎設施」的長線多頭地位。
在剛公布的財報中，四大雲端巨頭交出了令人驚豔的成績單。微軟的 AI 相關年度經常性收入大
增，Google Cloud 也寫下逾 60%的驚人成長，Meta 則透過 AI 優化廣告投放，拉升利潤率，亞馬
遜（AWS）的營收增速也創下近期新高。這些數據證明，掌握龐大生態系的巨頭，已經能將 AI 算力
成功轉化為自身業務的現金流。此外，根據 2026 Q1 財報數據，四大雲端服務供應商並未因高基期
而放慢腳步，反而持續加大投入，全年資本支出（Capex）預計由原先估計的 6,000 億美元進一步增
加至近 7,000 億美元。在這波驚人的支出中除了晶片設計商外，代工的台積電、供應HBM 的三星與
海力士，以及伺服器組裝與散熱解決方案的廠商都因此受惠，這也是硬體續漲的主要原因。
然而儘管 CSP 業者的支出續增，但投資人顯然不再全面買單，市場進入「AI 變現檢驗期」 。由於
龐大的折舊壓力與變現的陣痛 ，四大 CSP 業者在財報後的股價表現各不相同 。Google 與亞馬遜成功

用現有核心業務的強勁現金流護航 AI 支出，成為資金的避風港；反觀微軟與 Meta，因資本支出預
期大幅暴增 ， 市場擔憂長期支出深不見底 ，變現遙遙無期 ，積極的佈局反而成為短期壓抑股價的包袱 。
然而四大 CSP 的 EPS 年增率依然維持在雙位數，但評價面卻因資本開支焦慮被壓低，下檔風險相對
受控，這提供了一個逢低布局的機會。
AI 產業的長多格局不變，但在進入 5 月後，市場可能面臨傳統的「Sell in M ay」季節性逆風，
且 2026 年適逢美國期中選舉，歷史經驗顯示第二、三季往往是波動最劇烈的時期。因此，我們建議
半導體類股仍是不可或缺的持股，但也應該開始逢低布局美股七巨頭等大型科技類股。 七巨頭的預估
本益比已經回落至合理區間，且具備穩健的現金流。加上隨著 Google 的 TPU、亞馬遜的 Trainium
等自研晶片部署比例提升，CSP 業者將逐步降低對高價 GPU 的依賴，這將顯著降低長期推論成本。
因此建議適時布局相關類股，來迎接下半年變現能力轉強後的估值修復行情。

AI 電力類股：資料中心用電需求倍增，有利電力基礎建設發展
IEA(國際能源署)發布 2026 年 Key Questions on Energy and AI 報告，指出受到 AI 快速發展影
響，全球用電需求將急遽成長，有利於相關電力基礎建設發展。IEA 指出，全球資料中心用電量在 2024
至 2025 年間成長逾 15%，年增約 70 太瓦時至 485 太瓦時，占全球電力需求略超過 1.5%，未來幾
年用電需求更將進一步加速，IEA 預估全球資料中心用電將從 2024 年的 415 太瓦時倍增至 2030 年
的 950 太瓦時，屆時將占全球電力需求的 3%，AI 專用資料中心用電量更將成長三倍至 465 太瓦時。

驅動電力需求暴增的核心因素是 AI 伺服器功耗密度的指數級攀升。以 Nvidia 為例，2020 年
Ampere 架構機架功耗密度約 13 千瓦時，當前 Blackwell 架構已達 130 千瓦時，下一代 Rubin 架構
將躍升至 600 千瓦時， 屆時單一機架尖峰用電量將等同約 65 戶家庭 。在 AI 伺服器功耗上升的同時，
大型科技巨頭資本支出也持續攀升，為 AI 伺服器帶來更多需求，2025 年科技巨頭資本支出合計已超
過 4,000 億美元，2026 年預計再增 75%至 7,150 億美元，IEA 更進一步預估 2026 至 2030 年間資
料中心累計投資將達 3.9 兆美元，隨資料中心大量建置，AI 用電需求將隨之攀升。
此外，IEA 指出儘管單一 AI 任務的能耗效率正以前所未見的速度改善，單一任務功耗每年約可
降至十分之一，然而新興高耗能應用如影片生成、推理及代理式 AI 也持續推陳出新，這些新功能的
能耗可達到簡單文字生成的數百至數千倍 ，導致即便 AI 效率持續提升，整體能耗需求仍然有增無減。

在 AI 用電需求大增的同時，目前電力基礎建設供給端各環節均出現嚴重瓶頸，相關關鍵零組件
因產能不足，導致交期持續延長，包含變壓器交期平均需二至三年，燃氣輪機交期長達五年，電網併
聯在美國多數地區也須排隊長達五至十年。以德州 ERCOT 獨立電網為例，資料中心併網排隊量從
2024 年 12 月的 63 吉瓦時暴增至 2026 年 1 月逾 230 吉瓦時，但該州歷史尖峰負載僅 85 吉瓦時，
顯示電力需求遠超既有電網承載量。可預見隨電力需求持續攀升，相關電力基礎建設上中下游未來訂
單積壓量都可望保持高水位，並隨公司產能陸續開出，逐步轉換為公司營收貢獻。
展望後市，IEA 預估全球資料中心發電量至 2030 年將倍增至超過 1,000 太瓦時，其中再生能源
與天然氣各占約三分之一，分別達 360 及 340 太瓦時，核能預料將從 75 太瓦時成長至 120 太瓦時，
故各類相關零組件包含燃氣輪機、變壓器、核能及儲能等子產業都可望受惠這一 AI 用電趨勢，隨 AI
電力需求持續上升，對於各類電力上中下游供應鏈都可正向看待。

能源類股：海峽受阻導致油價居高不下；阿聯酋退出 OPEC 有利戰後供給恢復
西德州原油期貨價格 4 月再度攀升至每桶 100 美元之上，主因經荷莫茲海峽的石油出口持續受
阻，加上美伊談判持續未見明顯進展，同時期間內美國總統川普已數次拒絕伊朗所提出之協議，美國
對伊朗的封鎖行動也持續進行，故在全球石油供需缺口維持的狀況下，全球石油庫存水位不斷下降，
為石油價格帶來上行壓力。
國際能源署 4 月月報指出，受荷莫茲海峽供應中斷影響，3 月全球石油供應暴跌 1,010 萬桶/日
至每日 9,700 萬桶，其中 OPEC+產量月減 940 萬桶/日至每日 4,240 萬桶，非 OPEC+供應月減 77

萬桶/日至每日 5,470 萬桶，儘管沙烏地西岸、阿聯酋富查伊拉港及伊拉克經土耳其傑伊漢的 ITP 管
線替代路線出口量，已從戰前每日不到 400 萬桶提升至每日 720 萬桶，然而仍無法彌補荷莫茲海峽
的供給中斷，使得中東海灣國家石油出口損失合計仍高達每日 1,300 萬桶，自開戰以來，3 月石油供
給損失已超過 3.6 億桶，國際能源署更進一步預估 4 月石油供給損失將擴大至 4.4 億桶。
在供給大幅下降的同時，國際能源署也同步下修全球石油需求預估，預計 2026 年全年需求將年
減 8 萬桶/日，較上月報告下修 73 萬桶/日，其中 3 月全球需求預計將年減 80 萬桶/日，4 月進一步
擴大至年減 230 萬桶/日，合計第二季需求預估年減 150 萬桶/日，為新冠疫情以來最大單季跌幅，
下調需求預估主因荷莫茲海峽供給中斷，導致眾多仰賴中東石油進口的亞洲塑化廠面臨斷供，在缺乏
原料的狀況下被迫降低需求。
然而儘管目前荷莫茲海峽持續受阻，導致油價居高不下，然而戰後有利油價快速下行的因子也持

續在萌芽。阿聯酋 4/28 宣布退出 OPEC 及 OPEC+，為該組織近年來最重要的成員流失。阿聯酋目
前石油產能為 485 萬桶/日 ， 然而其受限於OPEC 配額 ， 實際產量僅約360 萬桶/日 ， 兩者差距達125
萬桶/日，過去幾年來阿聯酋持續因產量受限問題，而與其他成員國出現爭執，隨阿聯酋退出 OPEC，
將有利於其提升產量上限。雖然目前因荷莫茲海峽仍處封鎖狀態，阿聯酋石油出口同樣受阻，然而一
旦戰後海峽重開，阿聯酋將可不再受 OPEC 配額約束，立即釋放約 125 桶/日的增量供給，阿聯酋國
有石油公司 ADNOC 更規劃將在 2027 年將石油產量上升至 500 萬桶/日、2029 年再進一步上升至
600 萬桶/日。此外阿聯酋退出 OPEC 亦可能引發其他成員跟進 ，使得戰後油價下行風險將顯著增加。
展望後市，當前油價雖受到海峽封鎖影響，中短線上有望持續維持在 100 美元左右震盪，然而
美伊戰事未見進一步擴大，後續油價也難以再創新高，此外若未來美伊談判出現進展，海峽運量出現

緩解，阿聯酋增產也有利戰後原油供需恢復，故我們目前對於能源類股後市中性看待。

黃金類股：全球央行續買黃金，惟短線金價仍受美伊戰爭不利影響
黃金價格 4 月持續在區間內震盪，黃金類股表現也相對弱勢，主因受到美伊戰爭影響，油價上漲
帶動通膨預期攀升，使得市場對於聯準會降息預期下滑，帶動配置黃金的機會成本上升，同時避險
情緒導致美元走強，也不利於黃金等以美元計價的商品資產表現。此外，受到戰爭影響，土耳其動
用黃金儲備以支應外匯與流動性需求，短線拋售黃金也導致金價承壓。
世界黃金協會數據顯示，2026 年 3 月各國央行申報合計淨賣出 30 公噸黃金，主要就是受到土
耳其拋售 60 公噸所拖累，受此影響，合計第一季全球央行申報黃金淨購買量僅有 2 頓。然而若看加

計未申報買盤量，則是完全不同的狀況，根據世界黃金協會調查顯示，2026 年第一季全球央行黃金
購買量加計未通報買盤後，合計淨購買 244 噸，為自 2024 年第四季以來的新高，同時顯著高於過
去五年平均購買水準的 228 噸，顯示檯面下的世界央行購金趨勢仍未改變。
另一方面，在高盛 4 月 29 日發布的最新報告中，維持了對於 2026 年底黃金目標價 5,400 美
元/盎司的預測。高盛指出，目前金價主要驅動因素包括全球央行持續推進外匯儲備多元化、目前相
對偏低的投機性部位回歸常態化，以及聯準會預計在年內降息 50 個基點等三大支撐。在央行需求方
面，高盛指出，在其央行會議調查中，約 70%的受訪央行預期全球黃金儲備將繼續上升。在風險評
估方面，高盛認為短線風險偏向下行，主要因荷莫茲海峽封鎖若持續 ，可能觸發債市或股市修正，
進而引發黃金部位遭到清算， 然而中長期風險則偏向上行，因 伊朗事件與更廣泛的地緣政治發展 ，
如格陵蘭及委內瑞拉議題，可能加速資金轉向黃金配置。
另一方面，來自穩定幣發行商 Tether 的黃金買盤也值得關注。截至 2026 年 3 月底，Tether 為

支撐 USDT 儲備而持有的黃金市值上升至 198 億美元，約為 132 公噸，較 2025 年 12 月底的 126
公噸再增加約 6 公噸 ，此外 Tether 另持有 22 公噸黃金以支撐其黃金代幣 XAUT， 兩項產品合計持倉
達約 154 公噸 ， 此規模已足以排進全球前20 名黃金持有者 。JPMorgan 貴金屬研究主管 Greg Shearer
指出，Tether 的購金行為已對金價產生實質性影響，其 2025 年全年購買量僅次於波蘭央行，並超越
所有其他央行，顯示加密貨幣機構儲備已成為黃金新一個重要買盤來源
展望後市，中長線而言央行增持黃金的結構性趨勢未變，美伊衝突加上格陵蘭、委內瑞拉等地緣
政治風險，也都有望加速各國對黃金的分散配置需求 ，使得黃金有望獲得更多來自央行買盤的支撐。
然而短線上，在美伊戰爭尚未底定前，黃金機會成本上升及部分央行拋售壓力仍將壓抑短線走勢，
故預估黃金將在區間內震盪，目前對於黃金類股後市抱持中性看法。

### Page 10

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：華許入主 Fed 機率高，美債波動料加大
FOMC 在 4/29 的會議再度將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持在 3.50-3.75%。然而，理事 Miran 主
張降息一碼，而會後聲明中「傾於寬鬆」(easing bias)的措辭也遭三位地區主席(Cleveland 的
Hammack、Minneapolis 的 Kashkari 與 Dallas 的 Logan )反對 ，會議表決出現 4 票異議 ，創 FOMC
逾 30 年來委員立場最分歧的局面。前述三位地區聯儲主席反對寬鬆的措辭，導致市場對 Fed 後續降
息預期顯著弱化。

此外，川普提名的下任 Fed 主席 Kevin Warsh(華許)於 4/29 參議院銀行委員會審查通過，預計
於 5/11 當週進行全院表決。目前參議院共和黨、民主黨的議員比例為 53：47(含兩名傾向民主黨的
無黨籍議員)，由於除了 Tillis 以外的共和黨參議員均未公開就華許的資格表達反對，未來在參院全院
的表決中，華許也可望取得過半票數通過該任命案。若時程安排得宜，料可於 5/15 鮑爾主席任滿後
接掌 Fed。值得留意的是，鮑爾在 4 月的記者會表示將繼續留任理事會，這將導致未來的 FOMC 將
出現新舊主席並存的罕見現象。
綜觀華許的主要貨幣政策主張為「降息」 +「縮表」 ，兩者在貨幣政策方向呈現相反的效果，且
對不同天期的債券影響亦有差異： 「降息」對於雖使債券殖利率下修，但對短端影響較大；而「縮表」
量化緊縮(QT)雖屬貨幣緊縮政策，但堪薩斯聯儲研究(Smith & Valcarcel 2021)顯示，「縮表」推升期
限溢酬的幅度大於短期利率。兩者同時施行可能造成導致未來美債殖利率曲線淨陡峭化。話雖如此，

美國貨幣政策需要 FOMC 票委以多數決通過，前主席 Yellen 認為華許即便就任 Fed 主席，在表決中
仍難以取得足以支持其貨幣政策路線的票數。因此，即便 Warsh 上任，其政策主張(尤其在縮表的議
題上)短期將無法付緒實現，故預期在可見的未來對美國的貨幣政策走向改變有限。
然而，Fed 主席的權力在於議程設定與對外溝通，華許在聽證會上所表達的部分想法-包括降低
FOMC 會議與記者會舉辦的頻率等，將使 FOMC 決策透明度降低，或導致美國債市波動加大。其表
達改變目前聯準會青睞的通膨指標-核心 PCE 的意圖，也可能使金融市場的不確定性增加。
預期短線債市在通膨疑慮與政策不確定性的交錯下 ， 將持續面臨殖利率高檔震盪的壓力， 但考量
美伊全面開戰可能性極低，油價的通膨疑慮料隨時間鈍化，而 Warsh 立場傾向支持降息，因此就中
期來看，美債殖利率應有走降空間。同時，我們認為中短天期債相較於長天期債券殖利率相去不遠，
投資人無需承擔較高的存續期間更可享有不錯的債息收益 ， 且中短天期到期期間較短亦有資金靈活運

用的優勢，因此建議可以中短天期美國公債/投資級債為主。

Alchip Technologies Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Solid 2027 and 2028 outlook
with potential catalysts; OW
What's new? Ahead of Alchip's 3Q26 earnings release on November 6, investors are
looking for updates on the company's 2027 and 2028 revenue outlook, particularly
regarding AWS Trainium programmes and potential new customer wins.
Major projects – AWS Trainium: It appears TSMC is allocating additional 3nm
capacity to AI ASICs and CPUs in 2027. We do not rule out AWS-related revenue

growing more than 70% YoY in 2027, versus our current forecast of 56% YoY
growth. As a result, we remain confident in our 2027 revenue forecast of US$4.4bn.
We believe gross margin can remain sustainable despite the additional revenue
contribution, which would be better than our previous assumption of fixed gross
profit from 3nm Trainium3. For 2028, we forecast a Street-high US$8bn revenue
contribution from 2nm Trainium4. Our supply-chain checks suggest US$8bn–12bn of

revenue is possible if AWS allocates additional volume.
Potential new projects - Google KGD and others:  In  Asia-Pacific Technology: AI
Supply Chain: Google ASIC, who gets what for design services? (24 Aug 2026), we
highlighted key trends for Google TPU v10, including: "KGD (known good die) for the
compute die, which means Google owns the compute-die design and photomask
tools, while retaining flexibility to outsource backend design services for the

compute die." We believe Alchip is a leading candidate for this KGD backend-design
opportunity. In addition, Alchip is pursuing another US HPC customer opportunity.
Our checks suggest a switch-networking product is a possibility, potentially
involving collaboration with Astera Labs and NVLink Fusion.

### Page 3

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Rather than now slowing down, it’s actually getting stronger
TSMC reported an upbeat 3Q26 result with robust 4Q26 outlook. It guided for
4Q26 revenue of US$52.3–54.3 billion, representing about 14% QoQ growth and
58% year-over-year growth at the midpoint (based on FX at NT$31.8 per USD).
4Q26 GM/OPM is expected at 65–67% and 56.5–58.5%. TSMC reiterated its 2026
capex to be US$60–64 billion and said investment from 2026 to 2028 will be

significantly higher than in the previous three years, reflecting strong multi-year
customer demand, including agentic AI. It is also worth noting that TSMC plans to
begin using the High-NA EUV technology around 2030 and develop a 12-inch
mask capability by 2031, when it expects the tools to offer sufficient maturity and
cost effectiveness. Management said this timing does not undermine its
confidence in technology leadership.
Management described AI-related demand as exceptionally strong, supported by

accelerators, networking chips, and rising demand for CPUs associated with
agentic AI. TSMC’s previously guided AI GPU/accelerator CAGR to be mid-to-high
50%, but now sees more upside ahead. Given rising concern on AI expanding
peaking out, the management said it is clear that its capacity planning strategy has
considered customer forecasts, data-center construction, power availability, and
other supply-chain constraints. Management still judges demand to be above

available supply and is working to close the gap, while emphasizing disciplined
planning to limit the risk of overbuilding.
Figure 1. TSMC – 3Q26 results comparison
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Citi Research, Bloomberg
Capacity expansion plan and allocation
During the earnings call, TSMC also mentioned that despite the tight capacity
supply, customers cannot generally sublet or auction wafer capacity, while there is

limited case that collaboration is through mask-license agreements. For the
advanced packaging, TSMC has announced its Global Foundries and VIS’
agreement on interposer as part of its backend supply strategy, intended to help
ensure customers have enough capacity to bring products to market; It also
reiterated that TSMC’s CoWoS remains as the leading customer’s choice and is
already in volume production at 5x reticle size, while it is developing 9x and larger

solutions and evaluating CoPoS.

### Page 4

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Reiterate Buy, best position in AI arena
Our earnings projection is largely unchanged for 2027 /2028 earnings. While its
capex expansion, overseas operation and N2 will likely be a margin dilution factor
initially, due to its cross-node leverage, high UTR and yield rate, we believe its GM
downside is manageable. Overall, we see strong advanced node demand into next
year and the long-term order visibility will be supported for higher capex cycle.  We

maintain our TP to NT$4,000, based on unchanged 25x of 2027 EPS estimate;
reiterate Buy.
Key TSMC earnings call recap
n4Q26 guidance
–Sales: US$52.3-54.3bn (+14% QoQ at the midpoint)
–GPM: 65-67% (assuming USDTWD=31.8)
–OPM: 56.5-58.5%
n2026 full-year & long-term guidance
–Full year sales: raise 2026 full-year USD-based sales guidance from more
than 40% growth to +44% growth
–Full year capex: retain its 2026 capex guidance at US$60-64bn (70-80%
advanced process, 10% specialty, 10-20% for advanced packaging, testing

and masks)
–GM outlook
•4Q GM to be impacted by N2 and overseas fab ramps, but partially
supported by productivity improvement, UTR enhancement and
optimizing capacity allocation
•GM dilution from overseas fab would be c2ppts impact for the full-year
2026; with more new capacity expansion in the US, the margin dilution
impact would widen to 3-4ppts in the next few years.
–Long-term outlook
•Retains 5-year AI sales CAGR (2024-2029) remains at high-50% or above

nCapacity planning

### Page 10

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
10
TSMC
Company description
TSMC is the founder and leader of the dedicated IC foundry segment. The
company has built its reputation by offering advanced and "more-than-
Moore" wafer production processes and industry-leading manufacturing
efficiency. From its inception in 1987, TSMC has consistently offered the
foundry segment's leading technologies and TSMC-compatible design
services.
Investment strategy

We rate TSMC shares as Buy. We expect TSMC to deliver sales growth of
>40% in recent years thanks to strong growth from HPC/AI as well as stable
demand growth from smartphone, Io T, and automotive on tech migration and
a shortened replacment cycle. CPU outsourcing to TSMC offers further
growth potential. A rising cash dividend should also support the share price.
While TSMC has been increasing its capex meaningfully since 2009, its cost
structure remaisn competitive through continuous product mix

improvements, efficiency enhancements, and cost reductions.
Valuation
Our target price for TSMC shares of NT$4,000 is based on 25x our 2027E
EPS, higher than its three-year average forward PER, versus global peers’
average of 16-25x. We believe our target PER is justified by TSMC's leading
position in advanced process node and robust AI demand growth outlook.
Given its continuing leadership in the semiconductor foundry industry, we
believe TSMC’s stable order visibility and earnings outlook make it less

vulnerable to a potential global economic downturn than some of its peers.
Our target price equates to 2026E/27E P/E of ~ 36x/25x and 2026E/27E P/B of
~13x/10x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from achieving our target
price include: 1) weakness in the global semiconductor market; 2) larger-
than-expected margin contractions due to depreciation cost increases and
strong NTD; 3) competitors entering the foundry business; 4) longer-than-

expected digestion of inventory in the supply chain; and 5) a slowdown in
demand either due to a global economic downturn or a global trade
disruption triggered by tariffs.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

주가 추이

3Q 큰 폭의 판매 감소, 매출/영업이익 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 각각 44.6조원(-4.5% 이하 Y oY)과 2.4조원
(-5.4%) 기록하며 시장 기대치 하회 전망. 3Q 매출액은 글로벌 판매 부진 지속
과 환율 하락, 그리고 친환경차 판매 성장 둔화 등의 영향에 기인. 특히, 3Q 판
매량은 918.7만대(-11.5% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동기 대비 큰 폭 감소하며 매출
액에 부정적 영향. 이는 7월과 8월 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생
산 물량 차질이 발 생 한 부분에 기인. 파업은 기존 물량/믹스 개선에 기 여 했 던 
글로벌 HEV 판매도 둔화(+3.4%)시키면서 매출에 부정적 영향을 심화. 
3Q 수 익 성 은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입에 따른 원가 상승 영향과 친환경차 
판매 감소에 따른 부정적 믹스 영향 불 가 피. 이에 시장 기대치인 3.0조원을 크
게 하회할 전망. 다만, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지면서 판매보증비 
외화환산손익 영향은 다소 개선(0.4%p) 예상되며, 관세 영향 또한 전년 동기 
대비 약 1조원(2.1%p) 감소하면서 수익성 악화 영향을 일부 만 회 할 전망.  
’27년 지연된 신차효과 가시화로 반등 여력 충분 
4Q부터는 3Q 파업 영향 등으로 지연된 신차 확대 본격화되고, 현지 수요 높은 
차종 중심의 물량 확대를 통해 매출 회복 및 이익 성장 가시화가 기대. 아 반 떼
와 투싼 FMC 확대로 국내 및 해외 판매 둔화가 점진적 만회되고, 지난 9월부
터 터키에서 아이오닉3 생산이 본격화되면서 유럽에서의 시장 부진 회복이 기
대. 또한, GV80과 G80 HEV 모델 출시로 내년부터 국내/북미 중심 판매 확대
되며 올해 부진했던 물량/믹스 개선에 기여할 것으로 기대. ’27년 HMGMA에
서 HEV 신차 생산 투입 예정되어 있어 상 대 적 으 로 높은 수요가 지속되는 북미
에서의 HEV 현지 생산 확대로 가동률 증 가 와 관세 부담 완화도 수익성 개선에 
기여할 것으로 기대. 
부정적 환율 고려 필요하나 ’27년 강한 증익 기대  
’27년 동사 영 업 이 익 추 정 치 는 12.4조원(+22.3%)으로 ’26년 감 익 에 서 ’27년 
증익 전환 예상. 최근 환율 추세 고려 시 내년 상 반 기 까 지 부정적인 환율 영향 
확대 가 능 성 존재하나, 기존 노후 차 종 에 서 현지 수요 대응 가능한 신차 중심 
생산/판매 확대로 물량/믹스 개선되며 부 정 적 환율 영향 상쇄 기대.  
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-11.8%)과 상반기 적 용 한 코 스 피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)에 따라 목 표 주 가 를 670,000원으로 하향. 그러나, 연 내 RMAC 운영 
성 과 와 RA 설립, 12 월 모셔널 자 율 주 행 상용화와 웨 이 모向 로 보 택 시 
양산/공급 등 미래 사 업 에 서 의 가 시 성 도 여전히 기대 가능한 부분.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

## Document 217: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 265: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-05

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

Exhibit 1: Softer Cyclical/Higher Acyclical Inflation Keeps “Core” PCE at 3% Year Through August.
A) Cooling wage pressures ease cyclical inflation (housing, food services and accommodation,
etc.).
B) Surge in U.S. AI-related imports, tariff front-running, shipping disruptions and fuel- price
spike feed acyclical inflation.

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.
Investment Implications
While some key variables are
complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic
purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and
ongoing capital investment suggests
that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

ICE BofA MOVE Index is a market-implied measure of U.S. bond market volatility.
Consumer Price Index measures the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a market basket of consumer goods and services.
Baltic Dry Index is a daily shipping and trade index published by the Baltic Exchange in London that measures the cost of moving raw materials like iron ore, coal, and grain by sea.
Volatility Index (VIX) is a real-time market indicator that measures the stock market's expected volatility over the next 30 days.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

## Document 268: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research– BNP Paribas

## Document 310: France - 2027 Budget_ Fiscal consolidation must now resume (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/France - 2027 Budget_ Fiscal consolidation must now resume (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

2027 Budget: Fiscal consolidation must now resume

The 2027 budget, adopted by the French Council of Ministers today, calls for a reduction in France’s fiscal 
deficit to 5% of GDP in 2027 (down from 5.4% in 2026). This is a first step toward s the multi-year fiscal 
consolidation needed to stabilise the public debt-to-GDP ratio. This ratio is expected to rise further in 2027 
and until the deficit is reduced to 3% of GDP. Furthermore, given the increase in some expenditures (+0.6 pp 
of GDP for debt service and defence spending), the projected fiscal effort amounts to 1 pp. Nearly one-third 
of this effort would come from higher compulsory levies, and two -thirds from curbing government and 
social spending.

2027 Draft finance law: A step in the right direction. The government is forecasting an improvement in the fiscal 
deficit from 5.4% of GDP in 2026 to 5% in 2027. This projection is based on a credible growth assumption of 1% 
(see our analysis of French growth drivers). To achieve this target, compulsory levies (CL) are expected to rise to 
44.2% of GDP, an increase of 0.3 pp (the same increase as in 2026, compared with +0.9 pp in 2025). The public 
spending-to-GDP ratio would decline by 0.2 pp to 56.9%. The effort required to reduce the deficit to 5% is greater 
than it appears. Indeed, some expenditures will increase (interest payments by 0.4 pp and defence spending by 
0.2 pp). Consequently, the fiscal effort for next year amounts not to 0.4 pp, but to 1 pp of GDP. One-third of this will 
be achieved through increased CL, and two-thirds through spending cuts. Social spending will contribute, notably 
through the cancellation of EUR 3 billion in social contribution reductions for low-wage earners, as well as efforts 
totaling EUR 6 billion each in the areas of pensions and healthcare. Social spending shows the largest deviation 
from pre-COVID levels, with an increase representing 1.2 pp of GDP, nearly half of the gap between the 2026 
budget balance and a 3% deficit. Government spending, excluding interest payments and defence, is expected to 
be frozen in nominal terms.

A compromise seems likely despite a tight schedule. The French National Assembly will start reviewing the 
budget on Tuesday 13 October. The government will need to ensure that a majority vote against the draft budget 
does not form. The current circumstances, including increasing sovereign bond yields and the upcoming 
presidential election, call for a consensus based on fiscal consolidation of the size proposed by the government. If 
Parliament does not pass a budget by the end of the year, the government will have to choose between 
implementing the government’s budget proposal by executive order ( only possible if there was no parliamentary 
vote) or through a special law (as in 2025 and 2026). A special law would enable tax collection , while the 
government and social security agencies could continue to issue debt. In this case, the 2026 budget would apply 
in 2027 (without indexation) until Parliament passes a budget in the first few weeks of the year. This must occur 
before the end of February (when Parliament’s session ends, earlier than usual due to the presidential election).

The one-year postponement of the 5% budget deficit target has a measurable impact. The gap between the 
budget adopted for 2026 (deficit of 5% of GDP) and the actual budget outcome (deficit of 5.4%) is due to 
unforeseeable circumstances (the conflict in Iran  and extreme drought). However, postponing the deficit target  
previously set for 2026 to 2027 comes at a cost: 1) the public debt-to-GDP ratio is expected to reach 121% in 2027 
according to our forecasts (1 pp above our scenario with a 5% target in 2026 and a deficit reduced to 4.5% in 2027), 
and 2) the (upcoming) postponement to 2032 of the 3% of GDP deficit target (a commitment that most presidential 
candidates plan to make) rather than in 2029 (the government had committed to this with the European Commission 
in 2024). In 2032, the public debt-to-GDP ratio would stabilise at 124% of GDP (instead of 121.5% if the deficit had 
been reduced to 5% of GDP as early as 2026 and to 3% of GDP by the end of the decade).

Another delay in fiscal consolidation would further erode France’s credibility. This new delay could stem 
either from a decision by the government formed in May 2027 to immediately implement part of its agenda, or from 
the enactment of a special law that would remain in effect until (just after) the likely legislative elections in June  
2027  or  for  the  entire  year.  If  the  government  emerging  from  the  elections  were  to  resume  fiscal  
consolidation only in 2028 (with a deficit of 5% of GDP that it projects to reduce to 3% by 2032), public debt would 
reach 126% of GDP by that date (a one-year delay in fiscal consolidation would result in an additional 2 pp of 
debt). This further delay could potentially undermine the credibility of the next government, given that budget 
targets were  not met in three out of four years between 2023 and 2026, a situation that would be costly amid a 
sharp rise in long-term interest rates. Furthermore, a higher deficit would increase the amount of debt to be issued 
within an already  tight schedule. The Agence France Trésor (AFT) has announced debt issuances totaling EUR 
340 billion in 2027 (an  increase  of  EUR  20  billion  compared  to  2026).  This  increase  is  due  to  the  maturing  
COVID  related  debt (beginning in 2027 and continuing largely through 2030), while the amount of debt to be 
issued to finance the deficit will remain stable compared to 2026. A decrease from this last driver would be needed 
in subsequent years so that  the AFT’s debt issuance can stabilise.

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of the Advanced Economies Team, France 
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

## Document 314: FX Daily_ AI jitters have damaged USD safe haven appeal

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ AI jitters have damaged USD safe haven appeal.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

After big moves in front-end USD rates, the dollar has shown it
could be a laggard in further stock market instability if led by AI
concerns. Still, we don’t think we are on the brink of a major
USD decline. EUR/USD may struggle to stay supported today as
protests in France spill over into local assets, while we see
limited impact from US-Ukraine news for now

Francesco Pesole and Frantisek Taborsky
Markets will be closely monitoring AI, France and Ukraine this week
USD: AI jitters leave USD on less solid
ground
AI-related jitters entered the FX picture last week, although
currencies are more accurately borrowing the reaction from the
rates channel. The USD two-year OIS/SOFR has dropped 25bp from
the 14 October post-US CPI print. These are mammoth moves
considering they have been driven almost entirely by AI concerns,
with eﬀectively no macro input.

### Page 2

Markets appear to have a clear reaction function in mind: stock
market turmoil is enough to drastically reduce Federal Reserve
tightening plans, even with inﬂation running hot. The reasoning is
probably linked to the market wealth eﬀect that many believe has
underpinned US economic resilience. This is enough to suggest the
dollar will not be the safe-haven of choice and may actually lag
many other G10 currencies if stock market instability originates in

tech/AI.
Still, contagion to broader US and world equity indices has so far
been contained, while the Nasdaq drop has only erased the last 30
days of gains. AI-related sentiment will remain an input for FX this
week, but could easily fade if tech stocks ﬁnd some stability. From a
macro perspective, despite the repricing lower in US rates, we do
not feel this marks the start of a major USD downtrend. A December
Fed hike remains a likely outcome; policymakers do not appear to

be in a position to send a dovish message at the October meeting,
and still-elevated energy prices argue against a material recovery
in global bond markets.
A return to 101.0 in DXY remains possible over the coming weeks,
but we do not see much more downside beyond that for now. The
US calendar is rather light this week, while the FOMC is in its pre-
meeting communication blackout. For now, we are not observing
any obvious FX impact from souring US-Ukraine (and Europe)

relationships. Markets are probably quite cautious about pricing in
an imminent end to the Russia-Ukraine conﬂict or any longer-term
implications for Europe. Things could change in the case of
meaningful developments this week.
Francesco Pesole

now

has pushed our short-term fair value estimate for EUR/USD higher

to around 1.137-1.139. The current undervaluation is therefore
around 1.3-1.5%, broadly consistent with French ﬁscal risk

Canada releases inﬂation data for September today. The headline

particularly hot, especially given core measures have remained
anchored around 2.0%.
The jobs market has given some warning signals, with two
consecutive negative prints in August and September and the

CEE: Hungary to release its budget plan

Today’s focus is Hungary’s draft budget for next year. Hungarian
bonds have rallied strongly in recent weeks on growing
expectations that the plan will target a deﬁcit below 5% of GDP.

However, much of the good news now appears priced in, creating
downside risk if the government falls short of expectations. Even so,
the broader outlook for bonds remains positive, particularly
compared with this year’s 7.0-7.5% deﬁcit, and we expect investors
to buy any dip following a sell-oﬀ.
The region’s ﬁrst economic releases arrive on Tuesday, with Polish
wage, PPI and industrial production data. The main focus, however,
will be the National Bank of Hungary meeting. We expect rates to

remain unchanged at 5.50%, leaving attention on forward
guidance. Although the central bank has identiﬁed the ﬁscal plan
as a key factor for future easing, we believe cuts remain premature
and, global conditions permitting, are unlikely before the end of this
year or next year.
On Thursday, we expect the Central Bank of Turkey to resume its
easing cycle for the ﬁrst time since the US-Iran conﬂict began,
cutting rates by 100bp to 36.00%. September’s inﬂation data

provided a positive signal, while developments in investment funds
point to downside risks for the economy in the ﬁnal quarter.
Although crowded long TRY positions oﬀer less and less carry, they
remain popular in emerging markets, and we expect this demand
to persist in the coming months despite renewed rate cuts.
Beyond the scheduled events, we are monitoring political
developments in Romania, where the new government appears
highly likely to win a conﬁdence vote. This should help stabilise

## Document 351: OpenAI三款新模型被扒，Fable 5.5都快来了，憋半天就这？

Source PDF: all report pdf/OpenAI三款新模型被扒，Fable 5.5都快来了，憋半天就这？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

初步实测显⽰，三款模型在出图速度、画⾯细节和⽣成质量上存在差异，其中 Blue 表现被
部分⽹友看好，外界猜测其可能是 GPT-Image-3 的不同变体。⽬前 OpenAI 尚未官宣。与此
同时， Anthropic 疑似 Claude Fable 5.5 的测试演⽰也持续流出， AI 模型竞争再添看点。
摘要：
漏漏漏！ OpenAI 的图像新模型，⼜被扒出来了？？
有⽹友发现， OpenAI 的 API 列表中突然冒出了三个从未官宣的模型 ID ，统⼀代号 Aurora （极光）
——
分别是 aurora-red-alpha 、 aurora-green-alpha 、 aurora-blue-alpha 。
更亏贼的是，这仨已经悄悄混进 Arena 测试场，跟⽹友玩起了开盲盒 ——
能不能撞上全凭⼿⽓，但架不住⽹友们⼀个⽐⼀个能蹲，第⼀批实测 Demo 还真给薅出来了！
下⾯这位⼩哥，⾜⾜刷了好⼏个⼩时，终于逮到了蓝⾊隐藏款，并让其⽣成了⼀张⾬天男孩在
公园撑伞的图像：
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

还有⽹友还给 Aurora Blue 出了⼀道相当抽象的考题，让⼀辆⾃⾏⻋骑着鹈鹕。
然后 … 模型真让鹈鹕驮着⾃⾏⻋⻜上了天？？？
甚⾄ … 模型还贴⼼安排了固定背带，⽐起 Muse 的⽔上驮⻋版，多少更有点空中杂技⼤⽚那味⼉
了。（ doge ）
⼀番围观下来，不少⽹友开始猜测，莫⾮下⼀代 GPT-Image-3 ，已经偷偷开测了〜
不过，眼下还有个更抓⻢的问题。
隔壁 A 社的 Fable 5.5 也快漏成筛⼦了。
Open AI ，你是就想拿个图像模型跟⼈家对着⼲啊？？

### Page 3

三款神秘图像模型曝光，⽹友抽盲盒怒肝 Aurora ！
凡有⼤事，先在⽹上不⼩⼼漏⼀波， OpenAI 这套祖传操作，虽迟但到。
这回最先露馅的，是 OpenAI API ⾥突然冒出的三个陌⽣模型 ID 。
开发者社区出现了有关新模型的提醒，三个模型都以 Aurora 命名，并带有 alpha 后缀。

### Page 4

结果，熟悉的剧情⼜来了。
前脚刚被⽹友逮个正着，后脚模型引⽤就被撤下了。。。
本以为这仨要就此隐⾝，没想到⼈家转头换了个地⽅，直接混进 Arena 测试场，跟其他匿名模型
⼀起接受⽹友拷打。
⽽且玩的还是抽盲盒那套 ——
《抽到谁全凭运⽓，撞上 Aurora 隐藏款全靠命，欧皇请出列！》
于是乎，真就有⼀批⽹友真就蹲在 Arena ，哐哐刷新、疯狂抽卡，并晒出了第⼀波实测 Demo 。

### Page 5

先来看看最经典的画猫环节。
有⽹友拿 Aurora Red 和 GPT-Image-2.5 对⽐，让两边各⽣成⼀只绿眼睛的虎斑猫。
从效果看， Aurora 的构图更规整，猫咪的五官和主体位置安排得⽐较端正。
不过， GPT-Image-2.5 在⽑发纹理和⽣活化质感上似乎略胜⼀筹，看着更像随⼿拍到的真猫：
然后经过⼏个模型对⽐，⽹友得出的结论是 Aurora 三个模型中的能⼒中，感觉 Blue 模型最强，
其次是 Green 和 Red 〜
还有⽹友直接让 Aurora-red-alpha 画起了《死神》中的四枫院夜⼀。
从效果看，⼈物造型和神态还原度较⾼，复杂的蹲伏姿势也基本没有崩，⼿部、四肢⽐例和⾝
体透视处理得⽐较稳

### Page 6

画完⼆次元，再来点赛博朋克硬菜。
有⽹友直接让 Aurora-green-alpha 与 GPT-Image-2.5 正⾯ PK ，⽣成了⼀个坐在多块显⽰器前⼯
作的赛博朋克⿊客机器⼈。
从⽣成效果看，两款模型都完成了基本要求，但 Aurora 在机械结构、画⾯细节和⾊彩表现上更
出彩。
相⽐之下， Flare ⽣成的画⾯整体偏暗，部分机械细节藏在阴影⾥，视觉冲击⼒稍逊⼀筹？？

### Page 7

在看下⾯这位⼩哥，⽤ Aurora-red-alpha ⽣成了个超现实主义拼贴画。
卡通⿊猫、云朵墙纸、⽆限延伸的⾛廊等元素交错出现，画⾯既荒诞⼜有点诡异。
不同场景之间的衔接⽐较⾃然，⾊彩和画⾯⻛格也基本统⼀，整体透着⼀股荒诞⼜诡异的梦核
⽓质：
再来看看 AI 图像模型的另⼀项经典考题：像素艺术。
这次⽹友的要求倒是朴实⽆华，就让 aurora-red-alpha 画⼀根像素⻛⾹蕉。
别看只是⼀根⾹蕉，这类图⽚其实很考验模型对像素⽹格、描边和⾊块边界的控制。
从⽣成效果看，像素颗粒⼤⼩⽐较统⼀，⿊⾊描边⽐较清晰，整体还是有经典游戏道具图标的
质感的：

### Page 8

其他⽹友看了这根像素⾹蕉的效果之⾔，如果⽹格能保持清晰状态，那这模型确实有点东
西。。。
值得⼀提的是，⽬前社区⾥⼀个⽐较主流的猜测是这三款 Aurora ，很可能就是下⼀代 GPT-
Image-3 的不同变体。
之所以⼀下⼦放出红绿蓝三个版本，可能是因为正在进⾏ A/B 测试。（笑）
从已经流出的实测来看，不同版本确实表现出了⼀些差异。

### Page 9

⽐如， Aurora-red-alpha 的出图速度相对更快，但画质稍逊。
Aurora-green-alpha 虽然⽣成更慢，图像细节和整体观感却更好。
这也让外界猜测， OpenAI 可能正在测试不同的推理配置，看看如何在⽣成速度、画质和成本之
间取得平衡〜
Fable 5.5 蓄势待发， OpenAI 你拿图像模型接招啊？
Aurora 曝光的时间点，多少有点微妙。
因为最近⼀周， Anthropic 的新模型消息⼏乎没消停过。
10 ⽉ 11 ⽇前后，陆续有 Claude ⽤⼾发现，使⽤ Opus 5.5 时，部分请求似乎被路由到了⼀个能⼒
更强的神秘模型。
甚⾄，还有不少⽹友晒出疑似 Claude Fable 5.5 实测 Demo 。
⽐如下⾯这位朋友，展⽰了 Fable 5.5 通过编写 Python 代码、操控 Blender 制作 3D 超⼈动画的能
⼒ ——
还有⽹友晒出了 Fable 5.5 在 VoxelBench 测试中的表现，看完直接给出⼀个评价：怪物。
从公开 Demo 来看，模型⽣成了⼀处科幻⻛格的 3D 场景，包含发光传送⻔、墙体装置和复杂的空

间布局：
还有⽹友晒出了⼀张颇有独⽴游戏质感的 3D 花园 Demo 。

### Page 10

⾃⼰只让 Fable 5.5 在花园⾥创建⼀艘漂浮的⼩船，模型就⼀⼝⽓把⽯拱桥、树⽊、花草等场景
元素都安排上了：
这么看来， Fable 5.5 这边也是⼤漏特漏，⽽且⼈家传出的还是⼀款旗舰通⽤⼤模型。
相⽐之下， Aurora 如果只负责图像⽣成， OpenAI 这个节⻣眼拿它出来接招，多少有点尴尬。。。
不过，谁说 OpenAI 的存货就这仨了？？？（话锋⼀转）
就在 Aurora 红绿蓝三兄弟曝光后不久，⼜有⽹友在 Design Arena ⾥发现了三个陌⽣名字 ——
Aspen 、 Cypress 和 Maple 。
挺好，极光还没看明⽩，⽩杨、柏树和枫树⼜冒出来了。
⾄于这三款神秘模型究竟有何本事，与 Aurora 和 OpenAI 是否存在关联，⽬前还没有确切消息也
不好说。
偏偏这时候， The Information ⼜曝出， OpenAI 正在考虑⼀笔交易，试图重振 Sora ，同时进⼀步
加码机器⼈、创作者⼯具和个⼈设备等业务。
看来奥特曼最近⼿⾥的牌，还真不少。。。

### Page 11

《公債利率攀升，信用市場波動放大》
近期隨著公債利率攀升 、利率波動放大 ，成熟市
場債市信用利差皆有所擴大 ，惟目前利差水準距
上半年貿易戰擔憂時期高點仍有一段距離；不過
現階段貨幣政策不確定性仍將持續使利率波動加
大、市場重新定價貨幣政策預期 ，在今年剩餘的
幾個月中信用利差仍有進一步擴大可能；另一方
面預期市場將衡量債券發行人抵禦高利環境的能
力，故發行人獲利能力以及現金流的強弱或將
市場投資展望

美國非投資級債指數 正向/ 7.65 信評BB級以上之中大型企業債較為穩健
歐洲非投資級債指數 中性 / 6.10 違約率中期有望緩解，以現金流穩健發行人為主

重點摘要 左右市場表現 ，這將體現在高低評等發行人不僅基
本面的分化在高利環境下將更明顯 ，價格表現也將
更分歧。
《非投資級債信用基本面大致穩健，但分歧加大》
第二季美非投資級債發行人獲利狀況強勁 ，除交通
運輸、住房、電信和有線電視產業外 ，所有產業的
EBITDA(息稅折舊攤銷前淨利 )皆有成長，債務槓桿
出現六季以來首次下降 ，利息覆蓋率也提升至兩年
來的最高水準 ，但槓桿率和利息覆蓋率仍分別比指
數長期平均高出 0.17倍以及低 0.26倍，主要原因為
總債務成長 ，以及高利率環境下再融資成本更高 ，
令利息成本上升 。此外美非投資級債發行人過去 12
個月的資本支出年增幅創2023年第三季以來的最高
水準，主要由科技業帶動；不過在整體穩健的資產

負債表狀況中仍有 K型分化的情形 ，如43%的發行
人利息覆蓋率超過五倍 ，此比率為兩年新高 ，但同
時有21%的發行人利息覆蓋率低於 2倍，這也創了
近五年新高 ，反映在高利環境下債信基本面分化加
大，雖然目前違約狀況持穩 ，整體美非投資級債發
行人債信基本面在獲利支持下仍穩健 ，不過在利率
將在高檔區間維持一段時間的預期下 ，基本面的分
化有機會進一步加大 ，此外觀察過去歷史數據顯示
利息保障倍數與利率環境密切相關 ，如美歐信用債
利息保障倍數雖在2022年疫後復甦時期創近年高點 ，
但隨後又隨著利率走高而回落 ，因此儘管近期如上
述利息保障倍數出現小幅改善跡象 ，但基於美歐公
債利率的上升將增加債券再融資帶來的償債成本壓
力，預期債信指標未來也將承壓。
2

進一步加大

AI代理將推升科技獲利

科技產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/9/29
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《AI代理將持續推動科技與半導體獲利成長》
根據機構LSEG統計，受到AI代理推動GPU、記憶體、
客製化AI晶片，以及相關硬體獲利持續高成長 。機
構上調科技產業 4Q26、1Q27獲利年增率到 53.7%、
51.6%，本行樂觀看待科技產業發展後勢。
受到AI代理需求大增與大量低價的開放模型加入 ，
帶動2026年Token使用量呈現指數成長 。根據大型
語言模型 API聚合平台OpenRouter統計，週Token
使 用 量 由2026 年 2-3 兆 Tokens暴增至 9 月 129 兆

Tokens，短短9個月成長超過 40倍，顯示AI代理帶
動AI推論指數級擴張 。另外，Meta於9月發表個人
AI代理Muse應用軟體，可替用戶「管理郵件、訂餐
廳、填寫表格 、比價網購 、安排日程」，用戶關閉
軟體後會繼續完成任務 。預計也將有眾多企業在
Muse平台使用自己的 AI代理。機構統計Muse在開
放前12天美加 iOS下載量達 180萬，高於ChatGPT
同期的130萬，這代表AI推論需求仍將供不應求。

重點摘要 《AI代理與推論將帶動2027年半導體成長三成》
受到AI持續推動對資料中心 、高效運算 GPU/CPU
與HBM、DRAM、NAND等記憶體強勁需求 。機
構WSTS統計2026年上半導體市場規模達到 7,020
億美元、成長102%。其中記憶體成長高達302%、
邏輯IC成長45%。所以WSTS於8月上調全球半導體
2026年銷售額到 1.66兆美元 、年增108%(機構6月
時預估 1.51兆美元 、年增90%)，也同步上調 2027
年銷售額至 2.14兆美元、年增29%(機構6月時預估
1.91兆美元、年增27%)。記憶體和邏輯IC合計將佔
83%的半導體市場。機構預估2027年半導體銷售額
仍將由AI資料中心需要的相關晶片帶動 ，包含記憶

體將年增36%、邏輯IC將年增21%。
《博通上修AI營收預估，雲服務商與AI模型商持續
加大投資客製化AI晶片》

期，公司將2027財年包括客製化AI晶片( AI ASIC )
及AI網路的AI營收由1,000億美元上修至1,150億美
元，並預估2028財年AI營收將再翻倍至 2,300億美
元 ， 超越市場原先約 1,800 億 美 元 的 預 估 。
Anthropic和Google的TPU部署正按計畫推進 ，目
前博通已向Anthropic和Google大量交付了TPU v7
Ironwood，同時開始為 Google生產交付 TPU v8i，
預計本季將大幅提升交付 TPU v8i的規模。另外，
OpenAI和Meta的AI晶片專案也有所進展 ，公司已
推出OpenAI第一代客製化AI晶片Jalapeño。

零售客流量表
4
• AI 正從概念驗證轉向落地，並
產生可衡量的商業成果
《AI 正從概念驗證轉向落地，並產生可衡量的商業
成果》
9 越多家軟體龍頭不約而同揭露代理式 AI (Agentic
AI) 已由概念驗證階段跨入實質營收貢獻。益華電腦
(Cadence) 指出，其新推出的代理層採「消費加訂
閱」全新計費模式，且歷史經驗顯示效率提升不會
壓縮底層工具用量，一如 2006 年推出速度快 10 倍
的模擬器後客戶用量不減反增，原因在於晶片複雜
度呈指數成長，依台積電技術藍圖，未來 5 年晶片
複雜度將暴增48倍，人力根本無法同比例擴充。賽
富時 (Salesforce) 則與 Anthropic 共同推出
Claudeforce，並揭露頂級整合方案定價 550 美元，

較傳統業務團隊分散採購多套軟體之 1,300 美元支
出下降逾五成。近期財報數據顯示賽富時客戶加速
採用 AI 代理，AI 代理工作量加速成長並驅動相關
營收成長。MongoDB 的 Voyage 嵌入模型透過
Claude Code、Codex 等程式碼代理主動推薦，成
為全新的免費導流入口，單季即新增 2,900家 淨新
客戶；同時 MongoDB 也開始扮演前沿實驗室推理
記憶層角色，年化營收已達 5 億美元的語音 AI 獨角
獸 ElevenLabs 即因原生雲端資料庫無法承載代理
規模而轉單。Snowflake 則觀察到企業導入代理式
編碼工具後，資料遷移時程由過去以年為單位大幅
壓縮至一季內完成，帶動其由時間人力計價轉向成
果制計價的商業模式轉型。

重點摘要
零售客流量表
5
• 資安已從單純的成本中心轉為
代理式AI落地的先決條件，預
算來源正從單一資安部門擴散
至整體 AI 治理預算。
《資安躍居代理式 AI 時代最確定的新稅基》
代理式 AI 普及的另一面，是攻擊面與治理風險同步
暴增，資安支出因此出現明確的預算擴張訊號。
CrowdStrike 執行長明確表示，客戶端此刻是「資
安部門首度替IT踩油門而非踩煞車」，公司並預估，
AI 基礎建設支出將由 2.7 兆美元擴大至 2030 年的
6 兆美元，AI 偵測與應變 (AIDR) 滲透率終將如當年
的防毒軟體一樣趨近 100%。Palo Alto Networks
則揭露，其利用多模型弱點掃描，六成弱點由自研
模型找出、三成靠 OpenAI、一成來自其他模型，

印證單一模型無法涵蓋所有攻擊面，企業勢必得靠
平台化廠商整合多重情報來源。執行長並直言，一
般企業平均仍在使用 30 至 40 套資安工具，資料無
法互通，正是 AI 得以強化既有龍頭護城河而非顛覆
產業的關鍵原因。ServiceNow 與 Google 則分別
從身分治理與弱點修復角度切入：前者將 7 億台已
納管設備擴大至涵蓋逾 40 億台新設備，導入「零
常設權限」概念因應代理身分的短暫性；後者透過
Wiz 加 CodeMender 形成掃描、修復、驗證的完
整閉環，帶動逾九成財星百大企業採用其資安服務。
資安已從單純的成本中心轉為代理式 AI 落地的先決
條件，預算來源正從單一資安部門擴散至整體 AI 治
理預算，具備海量原生遙測資料的平台型業者，將
持續享有資料規模帶來的護城河。
大摩基準情境預估網安市值 2028 達 3 千億美元，CAGR 23%
資料來源：摩根史坦利，2026/9/20，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/09/22
(市值，十億美元)

## Document 458: ここが知りたい『投資信託販売チャネルの変化と現在地』

Source PDF: all report pdf/ここが知りたい『投資信託販売チャネルの変化と現在地』.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

投資信託販売チャネルの変化と現在地

資産運用調査部　主席研究員　大木 剛（おおき つよし）
　個人の資産運用において、 投資信託はNISAによる資
産形成やリタイアメン ト資金の運用などで、 重要性を増し
ている。 投資信託の販売では、 1998年12月の銀行窓販
解禁から四半世紀以上が経過した。 ここでは、 1999年以
降の公募投資信託の販売チャネル別の純資産額 （販売 ・
解約 ・ 時価変動が反映） と構成比率の推移を資料 １ で確認
したい。
　銀行窓販の解禁から1年後の1999年末時点で、 証券
は46.4兆円 （比率90％） 、 銀行等は2.8兆円 （同5％） とな
り、 その後も銀行等は預金取引等を背景とする顧客基盤
を活かし、 2007年末には34.0兆円 （同43％） まで比率
を高めた。 一方、 証券は45.3兆円 （同57％） と比率が低
下した。
　もっ とも、 銀行等の比率は2007～2008年をピークに
低下し、 証券の比率は上昇に転じた。 証券の純資産額に
は機関投資家も保有するETF （上場投資信託） が含まれ、
2010年代以降は日本銀行による大量買入れも統計上の
証券比率を大きく押し上げた。 資料 １ ・ ２ では、 個人向け投
資信託の動きをみるためETFを除いており、 それでも
2026年8月末時点では、 証券は159.9兆円 （同70％） 、 銀
行等は67.2兆円 （同29％） となっている。
投資信託の銀行窓販は証券にどれだけ迫ったか
　2009年以降の証券の比率上昇には、 証券各社の取組
みに加え、 いくつかの要因が考えられる。
　まず、 近年はNISAなどを活用した投資信託積立の拡大
により、 資産形成層を中心に大手オンライン証券会社の
利用が大きく増えている。
　また、 メガバンク等では、 顧客層に応じたグループ内の
銀行と証券の役割分担を強化し、 銀行顧客の富裕層や資
産形成層にもニーズに応じて、 証券がサービスを提供す
る動きが寄与している。
　さらに地域銀行において、 銀行の個人向け投資信託事
業を大手証券等との包括提携の下で共同運営する動き
も、 証券比率の上昇に作用している。
　一方で、 銀行等の純資産額も近年は堅調に伸びてお
り、 比率低下は、 銀行の落ち込みによるものではなく、 証
券の伸びが上回った影響が大きい。
　もっ とも、 「銀行と証券のどちらが高成長か」 という比較
より、 顧客ニーズに応じた役割分担の重要性が増してい
る。 2026年7月に政府が策定した 「金融戦略」 も、 インベ
ス トメン トチェーンの機能強化を掲げている。 多様な資産
運用ニーズに各金融サービスの担い手が強みを活かし
て応えることが、 資産運用立国の深化につながろう。
資産運用立国の深化に求められる取組み
資料1 公募投資信託 販売チャネル別純資産額推移

## Document 503: 三季度GDP增长回升至4.5%

Source PDF: all report pdf/三季度GDP增长回升至4.5%.pdf
Report source: 格林期货
Date: 2026-10-19

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

格林大华期货交易咨询业务资格：
证监许可【2011】1288号
成文时间：
更多精彩请关注公众号
三季度GDP增长回升至4.5%
摘要：
中国前三季度国内生产总值（GDP）同比增长4.6%；
三季度增长4.5%，好于市场预期的4.4%。9月中国出口同
比增长36.3%（前值增25%），社零同比回升至1.1%（前值
0.4%），1-9月全国固定资产投资同比下降7.2%（与前值
持平），均好于市场预期。9月工业增加值同比回升至5.5%
（前值5.2%），9月服务业生产指数同比增长4.8%（前值
4.1%）。9月房地产销售继续疲软，10月上半月30大中城
市商品房销售面积同比仍为负。三季度，全国规模以上工
业产能利用率为73.1%，继续在较低位置。前三季度，全
国居民人均可支配收入同比名义增长5.3%，人均消费支出
同比名义增长3.6%，居民扩大消费仍比较谨慎。近期，财
政部宣布居民房贷贴息政策10月1日起实施；央行宣布调
整完善若干货币政策工具，下调抵押补充贷款（PSL）利
率0.25个百分点，扩大抵押补充贷款支持领域；财政部安

排使用地方政府债务结存限额5500亿元，用于提高县区一
般公共预算保障能力和支持地方扩大有效投资。今年两会
制定的全年GDP增长目标是4.5%-5.0%，近期新的稳增长政
策陆续出台，我们预期全年将实现增长目标。

二季度增长 4.3%，一季度增长 5.0%。从环比看，三季度 GDP 环比增长 1.2%，二季度环比增

市连续回稳，二三线城市二手房价仍在筑底过程中。

9 月份，全国服务业生产指数同比增长 4.8%，比上月加快 0.7 个百分点。其中，信息传
输、软件和信息技术服务业，租赁和商务服务业，交通运输、仓储和邮政业，住宿和餐饮业

1-8 月份同比增长 4.7%。2025 年，全国服务业生产指数同比增长 5.5%。
9 月份，全国城镇调查失业率为 5.2%，比上月下降 0.1 个百分点，和上年同期持平。9
月份，31 个大城市城镇调查失业率为 5.2%，比上月下降 0.1 个百分点，和上年同期持平。9
月份，外来户籍劳动力调查失业率为 5.1%，比上月下降 0.1 个百分点，较上年同期高 0.2%。
9 月份，外来农业户籍劳动力调查失业率为 5.0%，与上月相同，比上年同期上升 0.3 个百分
点。
9 月中国出口同比增长 36.3%（前值增 25%），社零同比回升至 1.1%（前值 0.4%），1-9

月全国固定资产投资同比下降 7.2%（与前值持平），均好于市场预期。9 月工业增加值同比
回升至 5.5%（前值 5.2%），9 月服务业生产指数同比增长 4.8%（前值 4.1%），工业和服务
业生产均较 8月同比增速回升。9 月全国房地产销售继续疲软，10 月上半月 30 大中城市商品
房销售同比仍为负。三季度，全国规模以上工业产能利用率为 73.1%，继续在较低位置。前
三季度，全国居民人均可支配收入同比名义增长 5.3%，人均消费支出同比名义增长 3.6%，居
民扩大消费仍比较谨慎。
9 月 28 日国常会指出，针对当前经济运行中出现的问题，要加大宏观政策逆周期调节力
度。9 月 29 日，财政部等宣布居民房贷贴息政策 10 月 1 日起实施，有利于稳定房地产市场。
9 月 29 日央行宣布调整完善若干货币政策工具，下调抵押补充贷款（PSL）利率 0.25 个百分
点，扩大抵押补充贷款支持领域，增加科技创新和技术改造、支农支小等再贷款额度。财政
部近期安排使用地方政府债务结存限额 5500 亿元，用于提高县区一般公共预算保障能力和支

持地方扩大有效投资。今年两会制定的全年增长目标是 4.5%-5.0%，近期新的稳增长政策陆
续出台，我们预期全年将实现增长目标。

- 1 - 
個股報告 
矽格(6257) 
湖口二廠進入量產、中興三廠預計 27Q1 接續貢獻 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $327 
前次投資建議：2026.06.26 
「買進」 ，目標價: $301

報告日期：2026.10.12 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 26Q2 測試需求續強，湖口二廠產能爬坡速度與毛利率優於本研究部原預估，中興三廠預計
27Q1 加入。據此上修 26/27F EPS 至 9.9/13.6 元；目標價由 301 元上調至 327 元
(24x2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 15.5%，評等由「買進」調整為「逢低買進」 。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/10/07 收盤價： 283.0 ➢ 26Q2 合併營收 58.8 億元，季增11.1%、年增 23.1%，
毛利率 34.2%、營益率 25.6%，歸屬母公司淨利 12.2
億元、EPS 2.55 元。營收僅略高於研究部原估，惟毛
利率較原估 32.5%高 1.7ppt，為單季獲利優於預期主要
原因。26Q3營收 61.7 億元，季增4.9%、年增27.2%，
續創單季新高。 
➢ 26Q2 毛利率優於預期，主要反映AI與高階測試需求成
長、部分測試價格調升及折舊增幅有限；湖口二廠7月
量產後產能開出速度亦快於原預期，帶動下半年營收與
稼動率提升。AI晶片規格升級使測試項目及時間增加，
加上高階產品占比提高 ，也因此上修 2026 年獲利預估 。 
➢ 湖口二廠， 目前產能利用率約四成； 中興三廠預計26Q4
完工、27Q1開始量產。新增產能主要因應 AI、高階運
算及光通訊測試需求，湖口二廠全年貢獻及中興三廠新
增產能支撐研究部上修 2027 年營收。 
➢ 研究部預估矽格 26F 營收 238.7 億元（年增 21.9%）、
毛利率 33.5%、EPS 9.9元；27F 營收 298 億元（年增
24.9%） 、毛利率35.1%、EPS 13.6元。湖口二廠產能
開出快於預期，加上 AI 及光通訊測試需求成長、測試
價格調升，27 年中興三廠支撐營收與獲利續增；惟新
增設備陸續到位後折舊增加，毛利率擴張幅度可能收
斂，上調目標價 327 元(24x2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空
間 15.5%，投資評等為「逢低買進」 。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析 
矽格為台灣領先 IC封測廠，攜手台星科布局矽光子封測 
矽格(6257)成立於 1995 年，總部位於新竹，為台灣主要 IC封裝與測試廠商
之一，測試營收占比達90-95%，封裝占比約5-10%。公司提供晶圓測試 （CP，
占 30%）及成品測試（FT，占 70%）服務，並具備WLCSP、RF QFN 等封
裝能力，可提供客戶一站式 turnkey 服務。 
產品應用方面，2026 年 1 至 8 月依應用別，智慧手機占營收約 30%、網通
與物聯網 21%、AI／ASIC／矽光子／高速運算 24%、消費電子與智慧家庭
19%、車用與醫療 6%；公司積極調整產品結構，提升 AI、高速運算與矽光
子等高毛利產品比重，目標中長期 AI 相關營收占比達到與手機業務相當水
準。 
矽格透過子公司台 星科(6335)布局先進封裝領域，台 星科專注晶圓級封裝
（Bumping），目前 N3/N5 製程已量產、N2 完成認證。在矽光子領域，台
星科負責 Bumping 封裝、矽格負責測試，並與客戶共同開發CPO相關產品。
公司自主研發高速高頻 SoC測試設備（MAP），切入矽光子與 RF IC 高階
測試需求，提供客戶客製化測試解決方案。

圖一、矽格(6257) 2026 1~8月產品別營收比重。

資料來源：矽格，兆豐投顧彙整

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q2 毛利率優於預期，上修 2026年獲利 
26Q2 合併營收 58.8 億元，季增 11.1%、年增 23.1%，毛利率 34.2%，較
26Q1 增加 2.5ppt，營益率 25.6%，歸屬母公司淨利 12.2 億元、EPS 2.55
元 。 毛利率較研究部原估32.5%高 1.7ppt， 主要受惠AI與高階測試比重提高 、
部分測試價格調升，以及折舊增幅仍低於營收成長。 
2026 年 1至 8 月 AI、CPU/GPU、ASIC及矽光子營收 36.1 億元，年增43%，
占營收 24%，高於去年同期的 20%；記憶體營收 15.9 億元，年增 32%。AI
晶片腳數、功耗及傳輸頻寬提高，使測試項目及單顆測試時間增加，高階測
試營收增幅因而高於晶片出貨量。 
湖口二廠於 7 月開始量產後，產能開出速度快於研究部原先預估，26H2 營
收貢獻亦高於前次模型。研究部上修 2026 年營收由 233.0 億元至 238.7 億
元，毛利率由 32.5%至 33.5%，EPS由 9.46 元至 9.9 元。

AI 晶片測試時間拉長，高階測試產值持續增加 
2026 年 1至 8 月 AI、CPU/GPU、ASIC及矽光子營收 36.1 億元，年增 43%，
營收占比由去年同期 20%提高至 24%；記憶體營收 15.9 億元，年增 32%，
均高於公司整體營收成長。AI晶片腳數、功耗及頻寬持續提高，使測試項目
與單顆測試時間增加，帶動測試產值增幅高於晶片出貨量。 
目前 AI、ASIC及記憶體測試需求維持高檔，公司亦陸續調整部分測試價格。
隨高階測試需求增加，公司 2027 年 AI相關營收占比目標超過三成，仍為主
要成長來源。 
光通訊測試亦持續擴大 ， 目前測試範圍已由EIC延伸至 PIC及 Optical Engine 
(Insertion 1-3)，相關產品除電性測試外，亦需進行光訊號測試及光纖對位，
測試時間與設備要求高於傳統 IC。預期 2027 年公司光通訊測試營收成長優
於 2026。

湖口二廠開始放量，中興三廠接續 2027年擴產 
湖口二廠總建物面積約 8,500 坪，7月開始量產，10月產能已開出約四成，
目前月營收約 1億元；以約八成稼動率視為滿載，滿載月營收約2至 3億元。
依目前爬坡速度，預計 2027 年初達滿載，較研究部原先預估進度快。 
湖口二廠量產後已開始反映於營收。26Q3合併營收 61.7 億元，較 26Q2 增
加 2.9 億元，期間台星科營收下滑，因此營收成長主要來自矽格本體。湖口
二廠產能爬坡速度優於預期，不僅提高 26H2 營收，亦墊高 2027 年的營收
基期。 
中興三廠總樓地板面積約 1.1 萬坪，預計26Q4 完工、27Q1量產，設備將依
客戶需求陸續進駐，新增產能主要投入 AI、高階運算及光通訊測試。研究部
預估 2027 年上半年成長以湖口二廠全年貢獻為主，中興三廠則隨設備到位
逐季增加營收。 
母公司 2026 年資本支出提高至 88億元，主要投入新廠及高階測試設備；矽
格聯測 7 月另追加 20 億元，用於擴充美系 CPU/GPU 客戶測試產能。設備
到位速度將影響 2027 年新增產能開出/營收貢獻節奏。

- 4 - 
個股報告

台星科擴充晶圓測試，新增產能主要自 27H2貢獻 
台星科 2026 年上半年營收 27.2 億元，年增 20%，毛利率 27.5%。因應 AI
及 HPC客戶需求 ， 公司2026年資本支出由原規劃 56.48 億元提高至 134.27
億元，主要投入測試及晶圓級封裝相關設備；2027年 Bumping、Testing 及
Assembly產品線均有擴充需求，其中晶圓測試為主要擴產項目之一。先進
封裝方面，55×55mm FCBGA 已進入量產，並持續開發100×100mm 以上大
尺寸 FCBGA 及 2.5D、CoWoS相關製程。由於設備交期較長，新增設備預
計主要自 27H2 陸續到位，相關產能將增加 2027 年下半年營收；另一方面，
大幅增加的資本支出亦將帶動折舊提高，因此台星科對 2027 年集團獲利的
貢獻仍取決於新設備到位及稼動率提升速度，集團毛利率改善仍以 AI、光通
訊等高階測試占比提高及測試價格調整為主。 
圖二、矽格(6257)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

高階測試需求續增，產能擴充效益逐步反映 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 238.7 億元，年增21.9%，毛利率 33.5%、EPS 9.9
元；2027 年營收 298 億元，年增 24.9%，毛利率 35.1%、EPS 13.6元。 
評價方面，矽格 26Q2 獲利優於預期，湖口二廠爬坡速度亦快於原估，加上
AI、高階運算及光通訊測試需求維持成長，研究部上修2026/27F EPS 至 9.9
／13.6 元。前次報告考量AI Connectivity 與矽光子同步推升營收及毛利率，
將評價基準上調至 26x 27F PER，已位於公司歷史評價高檔；本次雖進一步
上修 27F 獲利，但考量 27H2 中興三廠及台星科新增設備陸續到位後折舊增
加，獲利增速與毛利率擴張幅度可能較上半年收斂，因此將評價調整至 24x 
27F PER。以 27F EPS 13.6 元計算，目標價由 301 元上調至 327 元，潛在
上漲空間 15.5%，投資評等由「買進」調整為「逢低買進」 。

## Document 564: 凱基投顧_6669 緯穎_向子慧_20261011

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_6669 緯穎_向子慧_20261011.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-11
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十月 11, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 電子 ‧ 台灣

緯穎 
(6669.TW /6669 TT)

客製化 AI ASIC 持續驅動 2027 年獲利強勁成長 
重要訊息 
緯穎 9 月與 3Q26 營收優於我們預期。我們預期 4Q26 營收將持續季增及年
增，並預期在 ASIC AI 伺服器與通用型伺服器營收增加，加上新客戶與新專
案帶動下，2027  年獲利展望穩健。  
評論及分析 
3Q26 營收優於預期；產品組合改善有利利潤率。緯穎 9 月營收達 1,726 億元 
(月增 20%、年增 100%)，優於預期，帶動 3Q26 營收達 4,346.2 億元 (季增 
56%)，較我們預估高 16%。此強勁表現應歸因於通用伺服器營收較佳，及  9 
月 ASIC AI 伺服器營收成長。9 月 ASIC AI 營收占比已超過營收的 50%，而通
用伺服器則維持穩定，3Q26 通用伺服器營收占比仍高於 50%，季增預估超過
20%。AWS (美) Trainium3 (T3) 占 3Q26 A I 伺服器(估 5k 櫃以上)營收幾乎全
部，而 9 月營收顯著增加  (估 3k 櫃以上)。營收成長及產品組合改善將有利  
3Q26利潤率。我們因而上修 3Q26 EPS 預估至 42.76 元 (季增 59%)。 
AI 專案與通用型伺服器需求帶動  2027 年獲利展望穩健。我們預期緯穎將於  
2027 年受惠於通用型伺服器與 AI 伺服器的需求及營收成長，主因多項 AI 專
案啟動所帶動。T3 Max (液冷) AI 伺服器自 4Q26 起開始出貨，1Q27 將量產。
預估液冷機櫃 ASP 可能為氣冷 PDS 機種的 2-3 倍，且緯穎應為 T3 Max 的唯一 
ODM。此外，緯穎供應 Meta (美) 的 AMD (美) Helios 機櫃，及供應 Oracle (美) 
的 VR200 機櫃，這些 GPU AI 相關伺服器預計自 4Q26 末開始貢獻營收，並於 
2027 年貢獻更為顯著。但 GPU 專案的變數在於銷售交易條件採買賣制 (buy-
and-sell) 或寄售制 (consignment)。再加上對新 Neocloud 客戶的潛在出貨，緯
穎明年 AI 伺服器營收擴張應相當顯著。而受惠於 代理式 AI與推論需求，及潛
在新增 CSP 客戶，我們預期 2027 年通用伺服器營收亦將成長，並為利潤率提
供支撐。我們預估 4Q26 營收將季增約 10%，毛利率約為 8.5%。受惠於營收
表現更強，我們上修 2026 年 EPS 預估至 139.76 元。考量 2027 年營收展望更
為樂觀，我們亦將  2027 年 EPS 預估上修至 223.38 元 (年增 60%)。整體而
言，我們認為客製化 AI 伺服器的強勁需求，將為緯穎帶來更多商機，如來自 
OpenAI (美) 與 Meta 等業者的訂單。 
投資建議  
我們維持緯穎「增加持股」評等，並將目標價重設 為 3,000 元 (除權後)，基
於 2027 年完全稀釋每股盈餘 200.01 元的 15 倍 (以 6.2 億股股本計算)。 
投資風險 
零組件供應緊缺；AI伺服器因寄售導致價格變化；AI伺服器專案遞延。 
增加持股‧維持 
  收盤價 October 8  (NT$)  2,240

前次目標價  (NT$) 7,750

調升 (%) 12.8

焦點內容   
1. 9 月及 3Q26 營收優於我們預期，受惠於
通用型伺服器營收較強，以及 9 月 AI 伺
服器需求強勁回升。 
2. 我們估計 4Q26-2027 年 AI 伺服器需求成
長，將帶動  AI 伺服器營收占比提升至  
50% 以上，進而推升 EPS 成長。 
3. AWS (美) Trainium3 Max 液冷式機櫃、供
應 Meta (美) 的 AMD (美) Helios 機櫃、供
應 Oracle (美) 的 VR 機櫃，以及新 ASIC 
專案，將推升緯穎 2027 年獲利。
